市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期 ，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力 。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於 CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高 ，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導 。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性 ，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者 CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破 1,140個基點 ，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場 。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期 。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量 ，經歷三年下滑後 ，整體經濟預計將在 2026年走出通縮 ，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性 ，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率 、消費者受通膨壓抑 ，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。
7月以來EPS和營收成長預期修正
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16
剔除能源、科技七雄和AI一籃子股票後的指數收益成長

顧
利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩 。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥： 受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法 (IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普 500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等 )，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議： 標普500指數短期內靠著半導體 /AI與能源雙引擎拉抬 ，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入 ，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升 ，以及終端消費力道的疲軟 ，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤 ，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中 ，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。
利潤率預測
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16
各產業第二季度收到的關稅退款
未來12月EPS預期修正動能與營收

顧
美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月 3.4%，其中汽油月增 9.1%、能源
價格月增 5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體 CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因 ，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升 ，成本傳導風險升高： 9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增 2.6%，能源價格大漲 10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升 20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源 ，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至 CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法： 通膨回升恐提高 Fed再度升息機率 ，並延長
高利率維持時間 ，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能 ，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現 ，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響 。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力 、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期 、高信用評等標的為主 ，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。
CPI 與核心 CPI 走勢變動

顧
供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫 。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市： 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求 ，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力仍 在，彭博表示 以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感 ，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14
通膨集中於能源和AI相關行業

顧
過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁 ，而且仍持續加速成長 ，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近 80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張 ，成為企業投資重要動能 ：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心 、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施 ，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求 。摩根大通資產管理指出 ，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約 5兆美元 ，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模 ，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利 ，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果 。
• 外部融資需求增加 ，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵： 隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資 ，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下 ，長天期利率面臨上行壓力 。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度 ，可能壓縮自由現金流與

資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差 ，並提高高估值科技股的評
價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率 、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性 ，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價 。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等 、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險 ，並審慎檢視高負債 AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動： IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮 ，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅： 隨金融體系數位化程度提高 ，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一 。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔： 美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示 ，市場風險偏好仍高 ，平均現金水位維持在 3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心 。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來 12個月經濟成長放緩 。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位 ，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源 ，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。

• 美國公債殖利率走高 ，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升： 調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至 6%時，
才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值 。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高 ，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估 523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健
任一月份定期定額進場投資試算
註：以MSCI全球指數2010/1/31~2025/12/31試算。假設定期定額每月月底投入、
原幣計算。指數資料試算僅供參考，非投資商品之實際績效。資料來源Bloomberg。
短進短出錯失行情的代價大
註：以美國標普500指數為試算範例，期間為1980年~2025年。資料來源Bloomberg。
投資人無法直接投資指數，資訊僅供歷史統計說明，非預測投資商品之績效。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

市場焦點
美國
公債回購
• 美國財政部9月9日宣布，將於翌日回購最高60億美元的10至20年期美債，低於部分市場期待
的80億至100億美元規模 。民間持有美債規模達 31.8兆美元，相較之下 ，本次60億美元回購
僅約占民間持債規模 0.02%，對整體影響有限 。此外，約6成的美債期限不足 5年，意味未來
再融資需求仍高，若財政赤字未明顯收斂，長端殖利率上行壓力仍存。
日本央行
• 日本第二季GDP年化季增率上修至 1.4%(優於初值1.1%)，儘管伊朗戰爭引發原油供應緊張給
經濟帶來壓力，但GDP增速依舊高於0.7%潛在經濟成長率，雖然經濟表現支持日本央行(BOJ)
逐步實施貨幣政策正常化 ，但更關鍵的催化劑來自日美兩國協調干預匯市 ，遏制日圓極端貶
值的政治壓力，彭博經濟研究預期日本央行將在9月會議上加息25個基點。

美國
通膨
• 美國8月生產者物價指數 （PPI）年增5.4%，略高市場預期的 5.3%，核心PPI年增4.6%，符合
預期。8月消費者物價指數（CPI）年增3.35%、核心CPI年增2.4%，均符合市場預期，但核心
CPI月升幅0.3%，高於市場預期的 0.2%。數據公佈後，FedWatch顯示市場預期9月升息機率，
從前一周的50%攀升至86%。

美國8月通膨數據(CPI)
• 美國8月通膨數據：8月美國CPI月增0.4%、年增3.4%，核心CPI月增0.3%，核心服務通膨升溫至0.5%，顯示通膨降溫仍不
均衡，能源價格上漲為主要推手，市場預估8月核心PCE月增約0.3%、年增約3.8%，仍高於Fed 2%目標，搭配強勁就業數
據，9月升息1碼的壓力升高。不過，本次通膨主要來自能源、需求及AI投資帶動，與2021～2022年供給型通膨有所不同。
• 市場反應與展望：CPI公布後，2年期美債殖利率一度上升，惟長天期殖利率反而走低，顯示市場關注焦點並非單純升息，而
是Fed能否維持抗通膨政策可信度。整體而言，升息未必等同Risk-off，若經濟與企業獲利維持韌性，股市仍具支撐。
資料來源： investing.com，2026.9.14

能源成本回升推升PPI，通膨壓力牽動Fed政策空間
• 能源成本回升，PPI重新升溫：美國8月PPI月增0.4%、年增5.4%，增幅均明顯高於7月，主要受到商品價格上漲帶動；其
中最終需求商品價格月增1.1%，能源價格上升4.2%，柴油更是大漲逾2成，成為商品價格上升的重要來源，惟最終需求服
務價格僅略增0.1%，顯示本次PPI升溫仍以能源及商品價格為主。
• 成本壓力仍在，利率政策不確定性升高： 扣除食品、能源及貿易服務後的核心 PPI月增0.3%，微低於7月的0.4%，但年增
4.7%，顯示價格壓力仍未完全消退；其中運輸與倉儲服務價格上升2.3%，反映部分成本仍具黏著性，致使市場對Fed利率
政策的重新定價明顯升溫，進而推動股、債市短線波動擴大。
• 市場觀察：由於本次PPI升溫主要來自能源成本 ，但服務價格壓力整體仍相對溫和 ，因此尚不足以視為通膨全面升溫 ，然

能源價格若持續走高 ，將提高Fed維持較高利率的壓力 ，建議投資人短線宜降低長天期債券與高估值成長股的追價 ，債券
可優先配置短中天期、高品質債券，股票則可關注能源、金融及具定價能力企業，提高投組對利率與成本波動的承受度。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics，Producer Price Indexes – Aug,  2026，2026.9.11

10月7日 至少40億美元
• 財政部對美債的回購規模不如預期：美國財政部9月9日宣布，將於翌日回購最高60億美元的10至20年期美債，主要鎖定流

動性較低的舊券，以改善長天期債券市場流動性。雖然本次回購高於先前設定的每次至少40億美元，但低於部分市場期待的
80億至100億美元規模。消息公布後10年期公債殖利率逼近5%，30年期殖利率亦突破5.3%。
• 回購規模在高額赤字背景下，難以有效拉低長天期公債殖利率：美國今年公債發行規模較2025年增加11.8%，民間持有美債
規模達31.8兆美元、年增8.2%，相較之下，本次60億美元回購僅約占民間持債規模0.02%，對整體影響有限。此外，約6成
的美債期限不足5年，意味未來再融資需求仍高，若財政赤字未明顯收斂，長端殖利率上行壓力仍存。反觀新興國家基本面
持續改善，且當地債市有貨幣潛在升值空間，可作為全球債券配置的分散來源。

全球主要市場及國家經濟成長率
新興市場
美股
日股
歐股
• 地緣政治動盪推升油價，但全球經濟依然展現韌性：美國和伊朗的衝突已經進入第7個月，能源價格上升及地緣政治風險仍
對全球經濟與市場信心形成壓力，但AI投資熱潮持續推升科技需求，使全球經濟展現優於預期的韌性。其中，亞洲仍是全球
成長動能最強的區域之一，2026年亞洲（不含日本）經濟成長率預估達4.7%，明顯高於全球平均2.9%及成熟市場1.6%。在
內需與科技出口支撐下，新興亞洲基本面相對穩健，具備較佳的中長期配置條件。

• AI資本支出延續，台韓成為核心受惠市場：AI資本支出持續擴張，2026年全球雲端AI資本支出預估達7,960億美元，並帶動
AI半導體市場至2030年突破7,500億美元。亞洲掌握全球半導體供應鏈核心，台灣受惠於先進製程與先進封裝需求，韓國則
受惠於HBM記憶體需求，台韓可望成為AI基礎建設擴張的主要受惠市場；同時，中國與印度則提供較穩定的內需及經濟成
長支撐。2026年亞洲企業盈餘成長率預估達72.13%，顯著高於全球平均，建議新興亞洲以台韓科技股為主要成長配置，並
搭配中國、印度市場分散區域風險。

短債供給增加未引發融資緊張，短天期債券防禦價值提升
• 回購市場維持穩定，短債供給暫未造成壓力：美國回購市場今年以來維持平穩，儘管9月短債償付與企業繳稅可能帶來短
暫資金波動，但SOFR與IORB利差仍處相對正常區間 ，顯示目前準備金與短期流動性尚稱充裕 。此外SOFR/IORB差值對
短期美債淨發行增加的敏感度明顯低於2025年底，反映流動性環境偏寬鬆。
• 貨幣基金成長放緩，供給吸收能力受到限制：零售資金流入偏弱，使貨幣市場基金資產增速落後 ，2026年迄今僅增加約
325億美元，遠低於2025年同期的2,050億美元；即便Q4季節性回升，新增資金對短債供給的承接能力仍可能有限。
• 後市觀察：年底前短債淨發行規模預估約3,250億美元，後續需關注短債供給、企業繳稅及財政部長端公債回購對資金面

的影響。建議投資人配置上可提高 1～3年期美國國庫券或短天期美債 ETF比重，以取得票息收益並降低長端殖利率波動
對債券價格的影響。
資料來源：US Treasury，FRBNY ，Federal Reserve ，2026.9.9

風險偏好升溫與債券供給增加，推升期限溢價與長債殖利率
• 全球期限溢酬上升，長債面臨重新定價：全球債券殖利率同步走高，不僅反映各國政策利率預期，也受到全球期限溢酬上升
推動；投資人對持有長天期政府債券要求更高補償 ，顯示長債殖利率上行已具有跨市場的結構性因素 。Bloomberg
Economics的模型亦顯示，全球期限溢酬是近年G7長債殖利率走高的重要共同因素。
• 風險資產吸引資金，放大長債供需壓力：AI投資題材推升股票市場、信用利差仍相對偏窄，加上大宗商品走強，使股票、公
司債及商品與政府債券形成資金競爭；在政府融資需求持續增加的情況下 ，若債券需求未同步擴張，政府公債需提供更高殖
利率才能吸引資金。
• 後市觀察：長端利率已成為股債配置的重要變數 ，若殖利率因財政供給與風險偏好維持高檔 ，長天期債券及高估值股票均可

能承壓；建議以短中天期美債及高品質投資級公司債為核心 ，待長端殖利率充分反映期限溢酬後，再分批拉長債券存續期間。
資料來源：Bloomberg Economics，2026.9.9
全球期限溢價因子推升債券殖利率上行
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

日本央行升息箭在弦上，但缺乏連續升息條件
• GDP修正支持升息 ，但關鍵點在配合日本和美國政府遏制日圓疲軟： 日本第二季 GDP年化季增率上修至 1.4%(優於初值
1.1%)，儘管伊朗戰爭引發原油供應緊張給經濟帶來壓力，但GDP增速依舊高於0.7%潛在經濟成長率，雖然經濟表現支持日
本央行(BOJ)逐步實施貨幣政策正常化 ，但更關鍵的催化劑來自日美兩國協調干預匯市 ，遏制日圓極端貶值的政治壓力 ，彭
博經濟研究預期日本央行將在9月會議上加息25個基點。
• 缺乏連續大幅加息的支撐力道： (1)經濟衝擊風險：日圓升值效應已對經濟產生緊縮效果 ，彭博預估可能拖累實際 GDP成長
達0.3%。(2)財政負擔過重：若政策利率升至 1.75%以上，將大幅增加政府利息支出 ，擠壓高市內閣的財政刺激空間 。(3)企

業投資放緩：私人資本支出仍顯疲軟(季比下降0.9%)，顯示經濟復甦基礎尚未完全穩固。
• 小結：日本央行在政治、匯率與經濟基本面交集下 ，9月升息已成定局，但考量財政負擔與全球金融穩定後 ，其後續加息步
伐應偏向謹慎與循序漸進 ，密切關注9月日本央行會議後聲明 ，若央行強調循序漸進而非連續緊縮 ，市場波動相對可控；反
之，若釋放過度鷹派訊號，則需防範市場拋售風險。由於市場資金對日圓套利交易依賴度仍高，日圓任何短期內的急速升值，
都極易觸發全球風險資產的連鎖去槓桿效應。

中國8月通膨回升，惟需求端疲弱
• 8月份 CPI及PPI：消費者物 價指 數 (CPI)月增 0.4%(前月月減 0.1%)、年增 0.8%(前月 0.5%)，生 產者物價指數 (PPI) 月增
0.4%(前月月減0.7%)、年增3.8%(前月3.5%)。通膨走揚主要受到能源及通訊設備成本上升影響。
• 觀點：中國自2023年以來，內需市場面臨房地產崩盤 、青年失業率飆升以及公共債務攀升等困境 。今年年初以來，通膨雖
然由負轉正，不過從細項得知，因國際油價飆升、AI硬體設備面臨漲價壓力，本波通膨回升主要是受到供給端影響。外部成
本壓力恐進一步壓抑中國內需消費，中國要在真正意義上走出通縮環境可能還需要更多官方政策發力。彭博預估，在需求未
出現明顯改善的情境下，中國央行需要近一步放寬貨幣政策，預測年底前可能降息10個基點。

資料來源：Bloomberg，截至2026.9.9。
受需求低迷影響，彭博預估通膨仍將放緩 8月能源及通訊設備成本上升
資料來源：Bloomberg，截至2026.9.9。

AI產業獲利亮眼，但需留意變現能力和資本效率
• AI獲利增速領先大盤，銷售成長成為獲利關鍵：AI板塊在2026年第一與第二季分別實現52.8%與59.7%的高獲利成長，受高
基期影響，未來四季度平均獲利成長預計放緩至 34.4%，但仍大幅優於非 AI板塊12.3%。預計2026年第三季至2027年第二
季，AI平均銷售成長率將達 27.2%，為非AI成分股(5%)5倍以上 。隨著未來利潤率擴張放緩 (預計未來 6個季度平均僅提升
1.66%，低於過去12個季度的3.52%)，持續的銷售成長是維持獲利的關鍵。
• AI供應鏈內部發展並不均衡： 半導體最為強勁，2026Q3至2027Q2預期銷售成長65.8%，獲利大增112.1%且淨利潤率大幅
擴張8.86%，為產業鏈核心引擎。雲端巨頭表現穩健，營收與獲利增速維持約26%，基礎設施軟體與硬體也有15%~20%營

收與獲利成長 。但應用端軟體成長相對乏力 ，未來四季度獲利預期增速僅 4.3%與6.5%，表現低於標普500非AI股平均水準。
• 資本強度創新高 ，但自由現金流承壓： AI資本支出佔營收比重自 2024年Q1的8.9%一路攀升至 2026年Q2的20.1%，預計
2026年下半年將達到23%的高點。資本支出激增導致自由現金流與資本支出之比(FCF/Capex)從2.48倍滑落至0.69倍，自由
現金流利潤率亦從21%降至13.9%。隨著現金逐漸消耗，企業可能需透過債券市場進行發債融資 ，以維持龐大基礎設施投資。
• 小結與投資建議：AI板塊依然是美股獲利驅動力，但隨利潤率提升空間受限、自由現金流受資本支出壓制，投資邏輯已從全
面暴漲轉向注重變現能力與資本效率。投資建議聚焦上游硬體與半導體，建議持續優選具備高定價能力與實質訂單支撐的半
導體與雲端基礎設施龍頭，留意資本支出佔比過高且自由現金流急劇惡化公司，優先選擇現金流充沛、融資成本低企業。
標普500指數盈利成長：AI vs. 非AI
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.7

標普500指數剔除AI後利潤率或下降
AI股票：自由現金流 vs. 資本支出

歐央9月升息一碼，措辭更鷹經濟預測上修，12月或再升息
• 歐洲央行9月升息一碼：歐央將三大政策利率各上調25個基點，存款利率升至
2.5%，反映能源價格受地緣政治因素推升，同時上調2027年和2028年的核心
通膨預測分別上修了 0.1個百分點。與此同時，對於經濟成長前景更為樂觀 ，
上調了今年的GDP成長預測，主要反映了2026年第二季數據優於預期以及下
半年內需表現更為穩健 ，此外，年末成長動能的增強為 2027年創造了更有利
的延續效應，也推動了明年經濟成長預期的上調。
• 投資建議： 此次會後 聲明基調偏鷹 ，強調通膨衝擊預計還將延續 ，市場預期
12月或再升息 ，這將有助銀行業利差 ，進一步支撐營收與獲利表現 ，同時，
企業投資與融資需求回升，加上製造業景氣改善帶動放款成長，均有助於歐洲

銀行業擴大收益來源， 建議投資人可以適度配置，增加投組多元性。

近五年美國8月CPI年增率
燃料成本料推動8月運輸成本上升
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
• 美國8月通膨數據：8月美國CPI月增0.4%、年增3.4%，核心CPI月增0.3%，核心服務通膨升溫至0.5%，顯示通膨降溫仍不均衡，
能源價格上漲為主要推手，市場預估8月核心PCE月增約0.3%、年增約3.8%，仍高於Fed 2%目標，搭配強勁就業數據，9月升息
1碼的壓力升高。不過，本次通膨主要來自能源、需求及AI投資帶動，與2021～2022年供給型通膨有所不同。
• 市場反應與展望：CPI公布後，2年期美債殖利率一度上升，惟長天期殖利率反而走低，顯示市場關注焦點並非單純升息，而是

Fed能否維持抗通膨政策可信度。整體而言，升息未必等同Risk-off，若經濟與企業獲利維持韌性，股市仍具支撐。

3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

## Document 079: 261006_daiwa_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/261006_daiwa_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We believe an improving product mix, driven by rising 
Nvidia’s CPU contribution, to be a key near-term share price catalyst.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. After Kinsus released its August 
preliminary results on 23 September, we note that we had underestimated 
the 3Q26E gross margin expansion on product optimisation and price hike 
scale. The results imply a GM of 33% in August (vs. 26% in 2Q26). We 
therefore lift our 3Q-4Q26E gross margin to 33.2-35.1% (from 28.9-30.3%) 
to reflect an improving product mix, including greater CPU substrate 
contribution. We believe the gross margin of high-end chipset substrate 
may be c.50%, supporting margin expansion. We also expect a c.10% QoQ 
price hike for both BT and ABF substrate in 3Q26, above Kinsus’ guidance 
of a high-single-digit% QoQ, making these substrates the other growth 
driver. We see further gross margin expansion in 2027-28 on the ongoing 
price hike trend and increasing high-end ABF substrate product mix.

Business outlook. We believe several favourable trends lie ahead. First, 
we see a rising contribution from high-end GPU and CPU ABF substrates. 
We believe Nvidia’s CPU currently accounts for 5-10% of total revenue, 
with Kinsus holding a 70-80% market share. We also expect Kinsus to 
acquire a teens% market share in AMD’s Venice, which should gradually 
ramp up from 4Q26. These will likely become key growth drivers of the ABF 
substrate segment in 2027, in our view. We believe the ramp-up progress 
for GPU substrate for these two clients is slightly slower than that for CPU. 
Second, we believe Kinsus will benefit from the rising BT substrate demand 
for optical transceivers. In our view, migration to on-board optics (OBO) and 
NPO/CPO will require ABF substrate beneath the optics engine (OE) in 
OBO; the OE and IC will need a bigger ABF substrate as we move to the 
NPO/CPO generation. As such, we expect the total ABF substrate demand 
to increase with rising adoption of optics in AI servers. Lastly, we expect the 
adoption of multiple-core ABF from 2028 to improve the rigidity and 
transmission performance before the official launch of ceramic and glass-
core substrate, which we expect to increase the ABF TAM as well.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 19-29% to 
reflect the better-than-expected gross margin in August. We reaffirm our 
Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP to TWD1,580, based on an unchanged 
target PER of 36x (high end of 3-year-trading range of 10-36x), applied to 
the 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 27-57% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish view on its gross margin expansion.

6 October 2026

3Q26 results preview: several favourable trends ahead

➢ Rising high-end ABF substrate likely to drive blended ASP increase   
➢ Multicore and optics likely to be the next growth drivers 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD1,580

Company profile  
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and mainland China.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 31 Jan 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Kinsus's leadership team comprises experienced professionals dedicated to steering the
company towards sustainable growth. This diverse board composition ensures a comprehensive
range of expertise and perspectives, contributing to Kinsus’s strategic decision-making and
corporate governamce.
Capital management 2
Kinsus maintains a robust financial framework, ensuring the availability of resources necessary
for operational efficiency and strategic investments. The company's financial health is regularly
disclosed through annual reports, providing transparency to stakeholders.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to stringent corporate governance policies to manage related party
transactions, ensuring transparency and compliance with regulatory standards. The company
discloses such transactions in its financial reports, maintaining accountability and stakeholder
trust. For instance, the company's annual general meeting documents provide details on related
party transactions, ensuring transparency and accountability.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Kinsus is committed to sustainable supply chain practices, emphasizing environmental, social,
and governance considerations. The company has established comprehensive ESG policies,
including commitments to labor standards, environmental health and safety, ethics, and conflict-
free minerals. These policies guide the company's interactions with suppliers and partners,
promoting sustainability throughout the supply chain.
E Data security 3
The company recognizes the importance of information security in its operations. As part of its
corporate governance framework, Kinsus is likely to implement standard data protection
practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance with relevant regulations.
Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official communications or contact the
company directly for detailed information on data security policies.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
September Sales +5% MoM/
+39% YoY
In this report, we focus on Bizlink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• September sales came in at NT$9,332mn (+5% MoM/+39% YoY)
• 3Q26 revenue reached NT$27,854mn (+20% QoQ/+50% YoY), slightly short
of MSe of NT$28,521mn (+23% QoQ/+55% YoY) by 2% while exceeding
consensus of NT$27,423mn (+18% QoQ/+49% YoY) by 2%.
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 revenue came in largely in-line with market expectations.

• Looking into 2H26, we expect HPC to remain the main growth driver, as
power interconnects should continue to see momentum from AI/general
servers. Meanwhile, data interconnects should grow along with new ASIC
model ramp-up and GPU server demand. Moreover, semi cap equipment
revenue growth should also sustain.
• We believe Bizlink has allocated increasing resources toward the optical
interconnect space, including the collaboration on ALC with Credo, mini

VCSEL optical module with ams-OSRAM and shuffle box with Corning. We
think that these opportunities could help offset a potential slowdown in its
AEC business as AEC solutions reach their scalability limits at higher data
rates, likely from  3.2T.
• Stay OW.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Alchip Technologies
Better allocation into 2027E; N2 project on track
Q3 sales up 243% QoQ on Trainium 3 ramp
Alchip reported September sales of NT$9,979 mn, up 14% MoM and 496% YoY,
concluding Q326 sales of NT$26,188 mn for +243% QoQ and 299% YoY, slightly
ahead of consensus, although below UBSe as we assumed a higher wafer allocation for
the Trainium 3 project, while sales contribution for its Chinese automotive customer and

NRE might also have came in lower than our expectation. We now expect Alchip's Q326
GM to arrive at 20.1%, down from 34.8% in Q226 on less favourable mix, leading to
OPM of 15.9%. We forecast Q326 EPS to grow 132% QoQ to NT$46.34.
Trainium 3  to sustain longer with potential upside; Trainium 4 tape-out on track
We expect Alchip's Trainium 3 monthly shipment run-rate to retain at current level into
H127F, and could extend into H227F with potential better allocation for Alchip with a

similar margin profile, subject to the final negotiation with its customer. We think
Trainium 3 deployments could stretch a little bit longer with a limited gap (vs prior ~3
months) before the next-generation Trainium 4 launch given the 3nm capacity tightness.
We continue to forecast overall Trainium 3 shipments at ~1.8mn units for 2026 and will
increase to ~2.8 mn units in 2027E, up 55% YoY, although Alchip's Trainium 3 shipment
could outgrow at 80%+ YoY given potential better allocation. For the Trainium 4 project

(2nm node), management noted it remains on track for tape-out before year-end and
still targets mass production by late-2027 or early 2028. Alchip continues to believe it
could enjoy a higher value-add given the increasing design complexity on chiplet and
higher wafer pricing for the 2nm node, while GM could retain at a similar level
compared with the current N3 project.
Engaging new customers for diversification
Alchip noted it remains in talks with another major CSP customer for potential adoption

of COT (customer-owned tooling) business model in the future for further cost saving.
We believe Alchip's competitive pricing,  strength for faster turnaround, and advanced
packaging knowledge put Alchip in a  good position to capture this opportunity, if the
customer decides to change the business model. Nevertheless, we do not expect
meaningful revenue contribution in 2027-28E from the new CSP customer. On the other
hand, we believe Alchip is in talks with other HPC customers for mid-size physical design

projects and this is likely to become a new revenue stream from 2027E. We estimate it
could book US$200-300mn revenue in 2027E from the new HPC customer.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with NT$6,600 price target
We lower our 2026E EPS by 11% primarily on lower Trainium 3 and automotive
contribution. We raise 2027E/28E EPS by 2%/5% to reflect  revenue contribution from
new HPC customer, offset by higher opex. We keep our PT unchanged at NT$6,600,
based on the same 30x 2027E EPS (vs. a 15-50x PE range). Stock is trading at ~20x 2027

P/E, below its peers and past five year average of 30x.

Company Description
Alchip was founded in 2002 by a group of Cadence managers with 20+ years of experience in
the semiconductor industry. It is an IC design and production service provider, and
management believes it can leverage third-party IP to enable customized chipset solutions for

customers cost effectively and fast to market. The company was funded by Cisco Investment
and Softbank Investment with VC funding in early 2000, then had its IPO on the emerging
board in Taiwan in 2010 and the main board in 2014.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Alchip using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced node project diversification and
ramp, and growing competition in advanced projects from other IC design service companies.

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS

be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.

downside risk to earnings, 5 = upside skew risk to earnings, N/A = no view)

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 4

BT substrate supply/demand gap at 11%/14% in 2027/28E on the back of solid memory demand

We introduce our 2026-28E BT substrate TAM forecast, expanding at a 32% 2026-28E 
CAGR to reach US$16.8bn in 2028E (up from US$7.4bn in 2025; Exhibit 1). This growth is 
primarily driven by solid memory demand in general servers (general servers will account 
for 32% of total BT TAM in 2028E, up from 21% in 2025; Exhibit 2), with increasing 
memory capacity/BT content per server. We expect the memory BT substrate TAM to 
outpace the broader BT substrate TAM with a 41% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 77% 
of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (was 56% in 2025). 
On the other hand, we expect the capacity growth rate to decelerate vs. the previous 
upcycle in 2020-22 (6% 2026-28E CAGR vs. 9% 2020-22 CAGR), as key Asian substrate 
suppliers are placing greater focus on their ABF substrate business, and are hence 
scaling back their BT substrate capacity (including Unimicron, which held 8% market 
share in 2025). 
As such, we now expect the overall BT substrate supply/demand gap to reach 
1%/11%/14% in 2H26/2027/2028 (Exhibit 3), while major BT suppliers have started to 
reduce capacity from 2026, potentially leading to a supply shortage in 2H26. We expect 
BT capacity utilization rates (UTR) to trend up amid a supply-constrained environment 
(surpassing 100% from 2027E per GSe), providing a better BT pricing environment going 
forward. The BT substrate supply tightness since the start of the year has directly led to 
a 10% YTD increase in overall BT substrate pricing, and we believe pricing momentum 
for BT substrates will remain robust in 2H26-2028E. 
Accordingly, we forecast overall BT substrate prices will further increase by 
15%-20%/10%-15% in 2027/28E, benefiting BT substrates’ profitability/pricing for 
Taiwanese players (GMs ranged from 15%-27% in 2Q26 and are projected to increase to 
18%-34%/19%-36%+ in 2027/28E; Exhibit 4).

AI; and (2) upcoming launches of new general server CPU platforms, which will drive

reaching US$16.8bn by 2028E (vs. US$7.4bn in 2025). We expect the memory BT

In terms of key end applications (Exhibit 6), we expect smartphone BT substrate TAM to 
grow at a 24% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 39% of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E 
(down from 51% in 2025; Exhibit 7). We expect general server BT substrate TAM to grow 
at a faster 42% 2026-28E CAGR, accounting for 32% of total BT substrate TAM in 2028E 
(up from 21% in 2025). Meanwhile, we expect AI server BT substrate TAM to see the 
faster growth among all applications at a 73% 2026-28E CAGR, reaching 9% of total BT 
substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025).

Solid growth in general server BT substrate TAM will support mid- to long-term BT 
market growth 
We expect general server BT substrate TAM to deliver a 42% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$5.3bn in 2028E (up from US$1.6bn in 2025; Exhibit 8), which will account for 32% of 
total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 21% in 2025). We expect general server 
memory BT substrate TAM to grow slightly faster at a 43% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$5.0bn in 2028E (up from US$1.4bn in 2025), accounting for 39% of total memory BT 
substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 20% in 2025). This is driven by solid general server 
shipments (we expect general server shipments to register an 8% 2026-28E CAGR; see 
here; Exhibit 9) alongside increasing memory capacity/BT content per general server 
(Exhibit 10). 
Our US analyst, James Schneider, expects Intel/AMD general server CPU shipment to 
grow at a 28%/36% 2026-28E CAGR, reaching a combined 59mn units in 2028E (vs. 
26mn units in 2025). This supports our constructive view that general servers are the key 
driver of the current BT substrate upcycle, given the strong correlation between memory 
BT substrate TAM growth and general server CPU shipment growth.

The strong growth in memory BT substrate TAM is largely driven by memory shipments

Strong AI server shipment growth in 2026-28E will also support BT TAM expansion 
We expect AI server BT substrate TAM to deliver a 73% 2026-28E CAGR and reach 
US$1,476mn in 2028E (up from US$160mn in 2025; Exhibit 14), accounting for 9% of 
total BT substrate TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025). We expect AI server memory BT 
substrate TAM to grow slightly faster at a 75% 2026-28E CAGR and reach US$1,428mn 
in 2028E (up from US$145mn in 2025), accounting for 9% of total memory BT substrate 
TAM in 2028E (up from 2% in 2025). This is driven by solid growth in AI server shipments, 
as we expect AI servers (8-GPU equivalent) and AI server racks (72-GPU equivalent) to 
register 17%/71% 2026-28E CAGRs (see here) with increasing memory capacity per 
server/per server rack. 
We expect GPU/CPU attach rates to move from 2x to 1.1-1.4x over time as agentic AI 
applications become mainstream, implying that each GPU deployment will require more 
server CPUs (see our US analyst Jim Schneider’s Intel initiation). Given that memory BT 
content in AI servers is primarily tied to server CPU count, if the GPU/CPU attach rate 
narrows from 2.0x to 1.1-1.4x, we estimate 1%/1%-2% upside to memory BT substrate 
TAM in 2027/28E. 
For AI server racks (72-GPU equivalent), BT content per rack continues to expand mainly 
due to increasing demand for more memory capacity, resulting in a 50% 2026-28E 
CAGR in BT content per AI server rack (Exhibit 15). A key upgrade is that Nvidia’s VR200 
will support higher DRAM capacity, implying a 100% increase in DRAM memory BT 
substrate content compared to GB200/GB300. We also note Nvidia is releasing a 
standalone Vera CPU rack designed for agentic AI, implying further demand upside for 
AI CPU shipments and memory BT substrates, as each Vera CPU rack incorporates 8x 
more DRAM memory BT substrate content compared to VR200.

### Page 9

Smartphones remain critical to BT demand with growth going forward tracking 
slower than the overall BT industry 
We expect overall smartphone BT substrate TAM to grow at a 24% 2026-28E CAGR 
(tracking slower than the overall BT industry CAGR of 32%) to reach US$6.5bn in 2028E 
(up from US$3.8bn in 2025), accounting for 39% of total 2028E BT TAM (down from 
51% in 2025). We expect smartphone memory BT substrate TAM to grow at a faster 36% 
2026-28E CAGR to reach US$2.4bn in 2028E (up from US$1.0bn in 2025), accounting 
for 19% of total memory BT TAM in 2028E (down from 24% in 2025). 
As mentioned in our previous update on the BT substrate industry (see here), we believe 
the previous upcycle (in 2020-22) was mainly driven by the proliferation of SiP/AiP 
designs that increased BT substrate content per device, especially for smartphones. 
While smartphones remain critical to overall BT demand, accounting for 51% of total 
2025 BT substrate TAM, we expect their contribution to moderate going forward 
(reaching 39% of 2028E BT TAM). This is driven by softer smartphone shipment volumes 
(we now forecast smartphone shipments at -10%/+2%/+2% YoY in 2026-28E; see here) 
and the fact that BT substrate content per smartphone will be mainly driven by higher 
memory BT substrate pricing (Exhibit 16) rather than other meaningful content value 
gains (memory will account for 46% of smartphone BT ASP in 2028E, up from 32% in 
2025). 
We expect 25%/26%/28% of overall smartphone shipments to be equipped with 
mmWave connectivity in 2026/27/28E (Exhibit 17), resulting in an 18% 2026-28E CAGR 
for smartphone AiP BT TAM to reach US$1,128mn in 2028 (up from US$713mn in 2025). 
For iPhones, mmWave connectivity is only available for models sold in the US 
(representing 35%-40% of total iPhone shipments per GSe). In the Android camp, 
Samsung’s flagship smartphones that support mmWave connectivity are also only for 
the US market.

Automotive BT consumption to steadily increase in coming years 
We expect automotive BT TAM to grow at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR and reach US$777mn 
in 2028E (up from US$317mn in 2025), which will account for 5% of total 2028E BT TAM 
(up from 4% in 2025). This stronger-than-industry growth is mainly driven by rising EV 
penetration going forward (EV penetration rate will increase from 14% in 2025 to 20% in 
2028E per our Japanese auto team; Exhibit 19) and the corresponding increase in 
electronics content per car, which requires more BT substrates. Furthermore, we believe 
rising autonomous driving penetration will result in an increase in electronic 
components/BT consumption per car.

BT market revenue to grow at a 28% 2026-28E CAGR with sustainable memory 
demand 
We project BT substrate market revenue to grow at a 28% CAGR in 2026-28E and reach 
US$14.6bn in 2028E (up from US$6.9bn in 2025). Despite this solid growth, total BT 
market revenue will account for less than 1% of customers’ overall revenue in 2026-28E 
(down from 2%-3% in the previous upcycle in 2020-22), as customers’ overall growth is 
tracking faster at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR. We believe this lower ratio of BT market

BT market revenue as a percentage of total customers’ revenue peaked during the 
2020-22 upcycle (at 2.5%-2.6%), when demand was primarily driven by smartphones 
(smartphones accounted for ~50% of total BT substrate demand during that cycle). As 
customers’ revenue has shifted away from consumer electronics toward AI-related 
applications, substrate spending has become increasingly directed toward ABF 
substrates for GPUs/CPUs/ASICs, while BT substrate spending is more focused on 
lower-end BT substrates for memory applications.

BT supply analysis: Major suppliers starting to reduce capacity, leading to slower capacity growth vs. 
previous upcycle

We believe overall BT industry supply growth in this upcycle will remain modest, as key 
Asian BT substrate suppliers are decreasing capacity, which will partially offset capacity 
additions from smaller players. Thus, we expect supply to grow at a 6% 2026-28E 
CAGR, compared to the 9% CAGR during the 2020-22 upcycle. This is mainly due to 
Unimicron transferring BT substrate capacity to high-end large-body-size ABF 
substrates (see here), while SEMCO is focusing more on expanding its ABF substrate 
capacity (see here) with its BT substrate business mainly focusing on memory and

smartphone AP (application processor). 
We expect BT capacity for Taiwanese suppliers to contract at a -1% 2026-28E CAGR, 
mainly due to Unimicron’s capacity reductions. We expect BT capacity for Korean 
suppliers to grow at a 3% 2026-28E CAGR, while mainland Chinese suppliers expand at 
an 11% 2026-28E CAGR. Overall, we expect Taiwanese/Korean/mainland Chinese BT 
suppliers to account for 27%/41%/16% of total revenue share in 2028E respectively 
(was 23%/45%/12% in 2025). 
The BT substrate supply market remains fragmented, with the top 5 suppliers accounting 
for 53% of total industry revenue in 2025. Over the next few years, we expect a slight 
change in the competitive landscape as major suppliers reduce capacity against a 
backdrop of robust demand expanding at a 32% 2026-28E CAGR, as outlined above.

We believe market players will increase BT substrate capacity by only 4%/3%/9% in 
2026/27/28E. Taiwanese players, such as NYPCB and Kinsus, mentioned they will 
primarily seek de-bottlenecking instead of investing in new equipment. However, ZDT 
mentioned it will expand BT capacity at its Qinhuangdao plants 1 & 2 starting in 2H26, 
and the company expects this expansion to increase its existing BT substrate capacity by 
20%. 
As for major mainland Chinese players, Shennan (002916.SZ; Buy; covered by Ting Song) 
will continue to expand its IC substrate business; the company expects Phase 2 of its 
Guangzhou factory to start production in 4Q26 (see here). Fastprint (002436.SZ; Not 
Covered), which will hold 3% of total market share in 2026 based on BBG consensus, has 
announced in June an investment of Rmb1.2bn for further BT capacity expansion; this 
translates to an additional 25k square meter monthly capacity in the coming years (vs. 
its current 50k square meter monthly capacity).

announced plans to invest US$1bn to further expand its substrate capacity in Vietnam, 
mainly for RF-SiP , BT and ABF substrates. For SimmTech (222800.KQ; Not Covered), the 
company plans to invest KRW105bn to expand BT substrate capacity specifically for

### Page 14

We have seen BT substrate demand improve since the start of 2026 alongside a firmer 
pricing outlook, and we expect demand growth to consistently outpace supply in 
2026-28E (demand CAGR of 15% vs. supply CAGR of 6%). We believe industry utilization 
rates (UTR) will continue to trend up over the coming years (reaching 100%+ after 
2027E) due to slow supply growth going forward. 
Memory predominantly requires wire bonding chip scale package (WBCSP) technology, 
and this production capacity is not interchangeable with flip-chip chip-scale package 
(FCCSP) technology (where smartphone APs represent the biggest demand driver). As 
such, we believe memory BT substrate supply will remain tight through at least 1H28, as 
new dedicated memory capacity will only come online in 2028. Overall BT pricing has 
already risen by 10% YTD, partially driven by higher material costs (including substrate 
CCL, copper foil, gold, etc., which account for 60% of total BT substrate BOM; Exhibit 
26). Going forward, we believe the slow increase in BT substrate supply, especially 
WBCSP capacity, will be the key price hike driver rather than simply reflecting the 
material cost increase. Furthermore, BT substrates typically account for less than 5% of 
total memory BOM, providing ample room for further BT price hikes amid the rising 
memory pricing trend.

### Page 15

With favorable BT substrate supply/demand conditions, we believe the profitability of 
key BT vendors will see a structural increase in the coming years. Similar to current 
dynamics in the ABF market, the BT substrate industry’s profitability profile has 
navigated three upcycles and three downcycles over the past 20 years (Exhibit 27). 
Overall margin profiles for key BT vendors have begun improving since the start of 2026, 
supported by increasing memory demand amid a supply-constrained environment, 
especially following several years of slow capex investment. We believe this profitability 
enhancement will continue in 2H26-2028, supported by sustained industry demand 
uptrend.

### Page 16

Optical module mSAP is seeing a widening S/D gap, prompting a shift of BT capacity to meet strong 
demand

We introduce our 2026-28E 800G+ optical module mSAP TAM forecast, expanding at an 
87% 2026-28E CAGR to reach US$7.1bn by 2028E (up from US$0.5bn in 2025; Exhibit 
28). This is based on our 800G+ optical module shipment estimates of a 48% 2026-28E 
CAGR (see here). The connectivity PCB within the 800G+ optical module requires mSAP 
technology, where several PCB makers have exposure to and are able to leverage their 
extensive mSAP experience from smartphone substrate-like PCB (SLP) production. 
On the other hand, major PCB and substrate suppliers are placing greater focus on this 
emerging business and pursuing proactive capacity expansion plans, especially ZDT and 
Compeq (the latter aims to ramp optical module mSAP capacity to reach monthly 
revenue of ~NT$3bn by end 2027 vs. less than NT$100mn as of 2Q26; see here). 
Although Unimicron was a front-runner in the 800G+ optical module mSAP business in 
2025 (29% market share in 2025 per GSe), the company is placing greater focus on its 
ABF substrate business, and hence leaving room for emerging peers to capture market 
share through aggressive expansion plans and technology leadership. 
We believe ZDT will become the market share leader in the optical module mSAP 
industry, as we expect the company to expand mSAP capacity by 5x over the next two 
years, positioning it well to gain share in a supply-constrained environment (our industry 
checks indicate mSAP is the most supply-constrained component in the 1.6T optical 
transceiver supply chain). Furthermore, the company currently holds an industry-leading 
yield of 90%+ for its optical module mSAP products. 
Overall, we expect the 800G+ optical module mSAP supply/demand gap to reach 
5%/25%/38% in 2026/27/28E (Exhibit 29). Although major mSAP suppliers continue 
to have proactive expansion plans, this appears insufficient to keep pace with strong 
demand. The 800G+ optical module mSAP tightness since the start of 2026 has directly 
led to a 20%-30% pricing increase YTD, and we believe the pricing outlook for 800G+ 
optical module mSAP will remain solid in 2027/28E.

### Page 23

Investment thesis, PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 39% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$1,025 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in line with industry downcycle 
average valuation). 
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market.

### Page 24

Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~118% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 1H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,515 for Kinsus is based on

19.5x 2028E P/E, which is +2.0x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.
Investment Thesis - NYPCB
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy rated on NYPCB

considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 26/42% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 2025), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s

high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (148%

2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s
earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,700 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We are Neutral rated on the
shares given long-term growth opportunity prospects: (1) over 70% of ABF shipments
are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and makes

price hike less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker than
peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its ability to
capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing more
favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see better pricing outlook
to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF account for 10% of the
company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers due to the

company continuing to lose market share to peers given its less favourable production
yield and slow capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our
outlook for limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally
positive view in the long term on its profitability trajectory.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,270 for Unimicron is

based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E
(10.3x).
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising penetration and AI servers ramp-up 
to drive growth ahead; Buy
19 October 2026 | 3:24AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +42% YoY/ 
+15% QoQ, driven by: (1) new AI server racks ramp-up, driving the overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in Oct (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 63k/ 110k/ 164k 
in 2026-28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks. (2) Its major ASIC AI 
server customer’s new model ramp-up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate. (3) Its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26. And (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit 
the lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s 
net income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain 
Buy.

### Page 2

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Sep revenue and revise down our 2026E net 
income by -1%, mainly on lower revenues in 4Q26E, reflecting the slow consumer 
electronics under high memory costs and AI servers model transition. We revise up our 
2027-28E net income by 4% / 0%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. Our 2027-28E 
revenue is revised up by 1% / 1%, mainly on a higher contribution from AI server liquid 
cooling components. 2027-28E GM are revised up to reflect the rising liquid cooling 
components driving profitability.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12-month target price and 
update our target P/E multiple to 30.6x 2027E EPS (vs. 30.3x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$4,736 (from NT$4,513). Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,736 on a target P/E multiple of 30.6x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

大立光短期營運受智慧型手機新機拉貨動能趨緩影響 ， 加上新產
品良率及綠電採購成本壓力 ， 預期第四季營收及獲利表現仍相對
保守。展望 2027 年，手機主鏡頭持續朝高規格升級，有利公司
發揮精密光學設計與製造優勢；另一方面， FAU 預計於 2H27
進入量產，隨 CPO 技術逐步發展，有望成為中長期新增營收來
源。 考量大立光具備高精度光學製造、 微米級對位技術及自動化
設備開發能力， 技術門檻高且具備跨領域應用優勢， 正面看待公
司中長期發展。綜合以上，研究部預估大立光 2027 年營業收入
為 706.92 億元，Y+17.08%，毛利率 51.37%，Y+4.53ppts，
營業利益率 40.04%，Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24 億元，
Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08 元。研究部考量儘管 2026 年消費
性電子需求疲弱，且 CPO 產品短期營收貢獻仍有限，然而進度
優於先前預期，隨新產品逐步放量，2027 年營運有望重回成長
軌道，給予大立光 38 倍本益比評價，目標價訂為 7,800 元，投
資建議為買進。

⚫ Largan 大立光電(3008.TT)公司簡介 
大立光電(3008.TT)成立於 1987 年，並於 2002 年台灣證券交易所
掛牌上市。大立光作為全球光學塑膠鏡頭領導廠商，主要業務內容
為研發與製造精密光學塑膠鏡頭，產品主要應用於智慧型手機，其
餘也包含平板、筆記型電腦、車用及其他消費性電子產品，在全球
光學鏡頭市占率約佔 30%。其總部位於台中市南屯區精密機械園
區，共有十座廠區及兩座興建中廠區。前十大股東持股合計
38.06%。客戶為 APPLE、華為、三星、小米、OPPO、VIVO 等手
機品牌廠，主要競爭者則為玉晶光 (3406.TT) 及舜宇光學
(2382.HK)。3Q26 出貨量佔比：20MP 以上約 10~20%、10MP 約

⚫ 產業概況 
記憶體成本飆升壓抑出貨，智慧型手機市場進入量縮價增 
根據研調機構數據 ，2026 年全球智慧型手機市場受記憶體供應短缺
及成本大幅上漲影響 ， 出貨量預估將Y-16.7%至 10.5 億支 ， 較前一
季預估的 Y-13.9%再度下修，並創下歷年最大年度衰退幅度，其中
2H26 出貨量更預估 Y-27.2%。 由於記憶體成本年增超過300%，品
牌廠難以自行吸收成本，陸續透過漲價及調整產品組合因應，帶動
2026 年智慧型手機平均售價 Y+27.6%至 581 美元，全球市場產值
反而 Y+6.3%至 6,130 億美元，呈現「量縮價增」格局。產品結構
方面，低階市場受到衝擊最為明顯，尤其100 美元以下機種因原先
毛利空間有限，品牌廠陸續縮減產品線，2Q26 該價位出貨量已年
減近 60%；相較之下，高階機種因消費者價格敏感度較低，且成熟
市場具分期付款等機制支撐，需求相對具韌性，帶動整體市場加速
朝高階化發展。 
展望未來，記憶體供需緊張預期至少延續至 2028 年，全球市場短

短，第四季營收動能仍具不確定性。毛利率方面，儘管新機生產良
率逐步改善 ， 然而綠電採購成本持續增加 ， 相關成本最快須至2028
年才能反映於報價，預期短期毛利率改善幅度仍將受到限制。綜合
以上 ， 研究部預估大立光4Q26 營業收入為 155.21 億元 ，Y-9.86%、
Q-0.83%，毛利率 46.65%，Y-1.32ppts、Q+4.44ppts，營業利益
率 35.29%，Y-1.46ppts、Q+5.04ppts，稅後純益 54.91 億元，
Y-18.28%，EPS 為 41.98 元。綜合以上，研究部預估大立光 2026
年營業收入為 603.80 億元 ，Y-1.26%， 毛利率46.84%，Y-3.59ppts，
營 業 利 益 率 35.06% ，Y-3.47ppts ， 稅 後 純 益 214.67 億元，
Y+0.90%，EPS 為 164.12 元。

手機主鏡頭規格持續升級，高階光學產品支撐營運動能 
展望 2027 年，智慧型手機主鏡頭將有規格升級，帶動高階鏡頭設
計提升，目前相關產品已完成技術準備並持續送樣，有望隨新機導
入逐步貢獻營收。潛望式鏡頭方面，下一波較大幅度規格升級預計
落在 2028 年，目前已進入試產階段，2027 年則以既有產品出貨為
主。獲利方面，因綠電採購成本持續增加，短期毛利率改善仍將取
決於新產品良率及產品組合變化。

FA 預計 2H27 量產，精密對位與整合能力開啟光通訊新成長動能 
光通方面，FA 產品已通過客戶認證，FAU 則持續進行送樣及整合
驗證 ，FAU 預計 2027 年年中完成產線建置 ，2H27 正式進入量產。
初期以單排 FA 及 Grating Couplin 技術為主，並朝 FAU 與 Carrier
整合出貨發展 ， 提高單一產品附加價值 ， 多排FA 及 Edge Coupling
則持續開發 ， 預計2028 年後逐步導入 。 公司憑藉微米級精密對位、
自製自動化設備及檢測能力 ， 可望在高技術門檻的FAU 市場建立競
爭優勢。預期 2027 年光通營收貢獻仍處於起步階段，隨後續產能
與良率提升，有望成為中長期成長動能。綜合以上，研究部預估大
立光 2027 年營業收入為 706.92 億元 ，Y+17.08%， 毛利率51.37%，
Y+4.53ppts，營業利益率 40.04%，Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24
億元，Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08 元。

⚫ 結論與建議 
大立光短期營運受智慧型手機新機拉貨動能趨緩影響，加上新產品
良率及綠電採購成本壓力，預期第四季營收及獲利表現仍相對保
守。展望 2027 年，手機主鏡頭持續朝高規格升級，有利公司發揮
精密光學設計與製造優勢 ； 另一方面 ，FAU 預計於 2H27 進入量產 ，
隨 CPO 技術逐步發展，有望成為中長期新增營收來源。考量大立
光具備高精度光學製造、微米級對位技術及自動化設備開發能力，
技術門檻高且具備跨領域應用優勢，正面看待公司中長期發展。綜
合以上，研究部預估大立光 2027 年營業收入為 706.92 億元，
Y+17.08%，毛利率 51.37%，Y+4.53ppts，營業利益率 40.04%，
Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24 億元，Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08
元。研究部考量儘管2026 年消費性電子需求疲弱 ，且CPO 產品短
期營收貢獻仍有限，然而進度優於先前預期，隨新產品逐步放量，
2027 年營運有望重回成長軌道，給予大立光 38 倍本益比評價，目
標價訂為 7,800 元，投資建議為買進。

## Document 250: Belgium’s public finances_ a missed opportunity

Source PDF: all report pdf/Belgium’s public finances_ a missed opportunity.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Belgium’s current failure to agree on a three-year €10bn
savings package is not yet a political crisis, but it is a missed
opportunity. A deal on the 2027 budget remains possible, yet
delaying the harder choices risks weakening the country’s
ﬁscal credibility as the 2029 elections draw closer
Philippe Ledent
Belgian PM Bart De Wever
This was supposed to be a decisive week for Belgium’s public
ﬁnances. The prime minister was due to deliver his general policy

statement to parliament on Tuesday, outlining a budget
agreement among the governing parties for the next three years,
up to the 2029 elections. In addition to measures already adopted,
the government is seeking €10bn in recurring savings, phased in
over the next three years.
A new timetable for negotiations
For now, however, the budget talks have failed to produce an
agreement. A central element of the package was a one-

### Page 2

percentage-point increase in the standard VAT rate, from 21% to
22%, but one coalition party ﬁrmly opposed the proposal. The
general policy statement therefore had to be postponed. Under
Prime Minister Bart De Wever's revised timetable, the immediate
priority is to secure agreement on the 2027 budget, including €4bn
in savings.
Ideally, a deal would be reached by the end of October, allowing
parliament to debate and approve the legislation before year-end.

This would avoid recourse to the system of provisional twelfths,
under which the previous year’s budget is rolled over on a monthly
basis to keep public services running. The PM has made clear that
he wants to avoid such an outcome at all costs. He is even prepared
to push through legislation that would trigger automatic spending
cuts of €3-4bn from January 2027 if no budget is agreed by year-
end.
In a second phase, negotiations are expected to resume with the

aim of ﬁnding a further €6bn in savings across the 2028 and 2029
budgets. This stage will be crucial for Belgium’s medium-term ﬁscal
trajectory. Postponing those discussions until next year is a risk. By
then, the federal and regional elections scheduled for spring 2029
would be closer, and the wide political diﬀerences within the current
coalition would make agreement on additional measures more
diﬃcult.
A chance to stand apart from France
There is no political crisis at this stage. With a package of measures,

Belgium should still be able to adopt a 2027 budget that brings the
deﬁcit below 5% of GDP. Even so, failure to reach a swift and
comprehensive agreement on the full €10bn package is a missed
opportunity for the federal government to show ﬁnancial markets
that Belgium is not France.
On headline indicators – public deﬁcits, debt and even economic
growth – little separates the two countries. France’s diﬃculty,
however, lies in its inability to set and pass a budget, compounded

by the political uncertainty surrounding the April 2027 presidential
election. Belgium, by contrast, has a governing majority capable of

## Document 266: CGコードが問う「取締役会の実効性」―「形式」から「実質」へ ～資本配賦・事業ポートフォリオ改革を巡る監督機能～

Source PDF: all report pdf/CGコードが問う「取締役会の実効性」―「形式」から「実質」へ ～資本配賦・事業ポートフォリオ改革を巡る監督機能～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

My Opinion

2026 年 9 月 29 日

1/6 
CG コードが問う「取締役会の実効性」―「形式」から「実質」へ 
～資本配賦・事業ポートフォリオ改革を巡る監督機能 ～ 
シニア・フェロー（非常勤）  水口 啓子 
（℡：050-5469-1527）

（要旨） 
○ 2026 年7 月に公表・施行された改訂コーポレートガバナンス・コード（CG コード）（以下、
コード）は、ガバナンス改革の実質化を重視し、形式的な対応にとどまらない、本質的なガ
バナンスの構築・運営を促している。プライム市場・スタンダード市場の上場会社について
は、コードの対象となる各原則について、実施するか、実施しない場合にはその理由を説明
するコンプライ・オア・エクスプレインが求められており、改訂コードを反映したコーポレ
ートガバナンス報告書の提出期限は2027 年 7 月末日となっている。 
○ コードでは、単なる制度や体制の記載にとどまらず、それらが実効性のある取締役会運営や
企業価値向上にどのようにつながっているのか、自社の経営環境や課題に即して説明するこ
とが求められる。コードは、単にコンプライとするだけでなく、具体的な取組みの内容や、
原則を実施していると評価する合理的な理由を説明することも、建設的な対話に資するとし
ている。 
○ コードは、取締役会の役割・責務として、成長投資や事業ポートフォリオの見直しを含む経
営資源の配分について、具体的な取組みを説明し、その配分が経営戦略に照らして適切かを
継続的に検証することを重視している。その実践に当たっては、どのリスクを取って成長を
目指し、どのリスクを抑制するかを取締役会が見極めることが重要となる。具体的には、設
備投資、研究開発、人的資本、無形資産等への成長投資と経営資源の配分について検証する
ことを求めるとともに、リスク管理体制に関する考慮事項として、サイバーセキュリティ、
地政学的要因によるサプライチェーン途絶、技術等の情報流出への対応を明記している。 
○ また、 こうした取締役会の機能強化に向け、コードの解釈指針では、独立社外取締役の役割 ・
責務、質・数・独立性の確保を重視するとともに、審議を活性化する観点から、取締役会事
務局（コーポレートセクレタリー等）の機能強化が追記された。 
○ 上場企業を取り巻く事業環境の非連続な変化に伴う新たな事業機会・リスクへの迅速な対応
の重要性が高まる中で、今回の改訂は、企業と投資家双方の取組みを通じてガバナンス改革
の実質化を促している。本稿では、コードの全体像を俯瞰した上で、取締役会の実効的な監
督が経営判断にどのように寄与し得るかを踏まえて、投資家との質の高い対話へどのように
つなげ得るかを考察する。

### Page 2

2/6 
1. はじめに 
2026 年 7 月 21 日に、コーポレートガバナンス・コード（CG コード）（以下、コード）の改訂版
が公表・施行された。2015 年の導入以降、2018 年、2021 年と改訂を重ねてきたコードは、今回の
改訂によって一つの転換点を迎えたと考えられる。改訂を経てコードは、 ガバナンス体制のあり方
や具体的な対応を細かく規定するのではなく、 各社が自らの経営課題や事業環境に即して、 原則の
趣旨・精神に沿ったガバナンスを実践することを重視する方向へ軸足を移す内容となっている。事
業環境が非連続に変化し、その変化のサイクルも短期化する中、 迅速な経営判断の必要性が高まっ
ている。コーポレートガバナンスを「形式的な体制整備」から「経営判断の質を高め、中長期的な
企業価値向上につなげる仕組み」へと捉え直すものといえる。コードの全体像と主な論点は、以下
の通りである（資料１）。

資料 1 2026 年改訂コードの構成と主な論点・着眼点 
基本原則 主なテーマ 主な論点・着眼点 
基本原則 1 
株主の権利・平等性 の
確保、株主との対話 
株主が判断に必要な情報を得て権利を行使できる環境を整える。特に、
有価証券報告書の株主総会前提出を重要な例として示すとともに、少数
株主の利益保護、政策保有株式の検証、株主との建設的な対話を重
視。 
基本原則 2 
株主以外のステークホ
ルダーとの適切な協働 
持続的な成長を支える従業員や取引先等との協働を重視。解釈指針で
は、人的資本への投資と適切な分配、公正・適正な取引を例示。多様性
の確保や企業年金のアセットオーナーとしての機能発揮も対象。 
基本原則 3 
適切な 情 報 開 示 と 透
明性の確保 
財務情報に加え、戦略・リスク・ガバナンス等の非財務情報を、ひな型的
な記述にとどめず、正確で分かりやすく有用な形で示すことを重視。開示
を株主との建設的な対話の基盤と位置付ける。 
基本原則 4 取締役会等の責務 
成長の道筋を示し、成長投資や事業ポートフォリオの見直しを含む経営
資源配分を説明・継続的に検証することを重視。独立社外取締役による
監督、取締役会事務局の機能強化、サイバー・地政学リスク等への対応
を通じて取締役会の実効性を高める。 
（出所）東京証券取引所「コーポレートガバナンス・コード～会社の持続的な成長と中長期的な企業価値の向上のために～」
（2026 年 7 月 21 日）を参照の上、第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

今回の改訂では、 取締役会が成長の道筋を示し、 成長投資や事業ポートフォリオの見直しを含む
経営資源の配分について、 具体的な取組みを説明するとともに、 その妥当性を継続的に検証するこ
とが重視されている。具体的には、①取締役会の在るべき姿について不断の検討を行うこと、②独
立社外取締役の質と実効性を高めること、③取締役会事務局（コーポレートセクレタリー等）の機
能を強化することなどが重要なテーマとなっている。なお、 今回の改訂では対話環境の整備として

### Page 3

3/6 
有価証券報告書の総会前開示なども明記されている。 
企業価値を左右する経営上の重要課題について、 取締役会がどのような問いを投げかけ、 経営陣
との議論を通じて意思決定の質の向上につなげるかが重要である。 今後は、 コーポレートガバナン
ス報告書においても、実質的なガバナンスに関する記載への注目が高まることが想定される。 
本稿では、取締役会の実効性を「取締役会における議論が、経営上の重要な意思決定の質や、企
業の持続的価値創造にどのように寄与し得るか」 という観点から捉える。2026 年に改訂されたコー
ドの全体像を踏まえた上で、取締役会における「監督」の在り方を整理し、その具体的な実践領域
として、資本配賦と事業ポートフォリオ選択のあり方を取り上げる。さらに、独立社外取締役や取
締役会事務局の役割に触れつつ、 取締役会の実効性をいかに高め、 投資家との質の高い対話へつな
げるかを考察する。

2．取締役会による経営の方向付けと実効的な監督 
コードの改訂が示唆するように、取締役会の本質的な役割は、会社の進むべき方向を示し、経営
陣がリスクと機会を多角的に捉えて適切にリスクテイクできる環境を整えるとともに、 独立した客
観的な立場から経営を監督することにある（資料2）。

資料 2 取締役会の役割・責務に関わる諸視点 
項目 主な視点・検討事項 
企業戦略の方向付け 
・経営理念に基づき、成長戦略・経営計画を策定する。 
・資本コストを意識し、収益性・資本効率の目標を明確化する 。 
・成長投資、M&A、人的資本・知的財産への投資、事業ポートフォリオの見直しな
ど、経営資源の配分を具体的に示す。 
・中期経営計画未達の原因と対応策を検討し、株主に説明する。 
適切なリスクテイクの支援 
・経営陣の健全な起業家精神に基づく 提案を歓迎しつつ、客観的・多角的に検討
する。 
・経営資源が成長戦略に沿って有効活用されているかを継続的に検証する。 
・中長期的な企業価値向上につながる報酬制度・インセンティブを設計する。 
経営陣への実効的な監督 
・経営陣の業績を客観的に評価し、必要に応じて選解任に反映する。 
・特に CEO の後継者計画（サクセッションプラン）を取締役会が主体的に監督す
る。 
・適時・正確な情報開示を監督する。 
・支配株主や経営陣との利益相反・関連当事者取引を適切に管理する。 
内部統制・リスク管理 
・グループ全体の内部統制・リスク管理体制を整備し、運用状況を監督する。 
・サイバーセキュリティ、地政学的なサプライチェーン途絶、技術 等の情報流出な
どのリスクにも対応する。

### Page 4

4/6 
サステナビリティを巡る取組
み 
・中長期的な企業価値の向上の観点から、サステナビリティを巡る取組みについ
て基本的な方針を策定・適切な対応を行う。 
（出所）東京証券取引所「コーポレートガバナンス・コード～会社の持続的な成長と中長期的な企業価値の向上のために～」
（2026 年 7 月 21 日）を参照の上、第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

実効的な取締役会を考察するに際しては、重要な戦略課題に議論を集中させ、経営陣に「有用な
問い」 を投げかけることが大いに期待される。 「何を取締役会が自ら判断し、 何を経営陣に委ねるの
か」という委任の境界を明確にし、経営陣の意思決定の前提、リスク選好、リスク抑制方針・施策
を多角的に検証することで、意思決定の質を高めることが期待される。 
中期経営計画の進捗、 主要な前提条件の変化などを継続的にモニタリングし、 計画が未達となっ
た場合には、 その要因を独立的な視点から分析して、 次の戦略や資本配賦に反映させることが肝要
である。持続的な企業価値創造に向けて、取締役会がリスクテイクを支援・監督する際も、ストレ
スシナリオを含め、 成長の源泉や市場規模の見立て、 競争優位性の持続可能性を踏まえた検討が必
要となる。加えて、上場会社のグローバル化がさらに進展する中、グループ全体のリスク管理・内
部統制の重要性は高まっている。 法的リスクや地政学リスク、 グローバル展開に伴う内部統制の実
効性に至るまで、多角的な問いを通じて、意思決定における前提、リスクなどを検証する姿勢が重
要となる。

3．資本配賦と事業ポートフォリオ改革の合理性の検証 
取締役会の監督機能が試される代表的な領域が、 資本配賦と事業ポートフォリオ改革であり、 投
資家にとっても大きな関心事項である。コードは、資本コストを意識し、収益性・資本効率の目標
を明確化することに加え、 成長投資、M&A、 人的資本・知的財産への投資、 事業ポートフォリオの見
直しなどについて言及している。取締役会は、どの時間軸で価値を生み出すのか、どの事業にどの
程度の経営資源を配分し、 リスク調整後にどの程度のリターンを見込むのかを、 全社的かつ多角的
な視点から判断することが求められる。 
事業ポートフォリオを検証する際には、ROIC（投下資本利益率）と WACC（加重平均資本コスト）
の比較が有用な判断材料となる。ただし、これらは取締役会が活用し得る指標の一例であり、その
比較のみで事業の継続・撤退を決めることは適切ではない。投資期間やリスク、将来の選択肢とし
ての価値に加え、将来の成長率、追加投資に対する限界リターン、構造改革の余地、事業間のシナ
ジー、撤退コストなども踏まえて、企業価値の向上に向けて評価することが重要である。様々な参
考指標が想定される中で、投下資本の規模を考慮した経済的利益（Economic Profit 、以下「EP」）
は、ROIC だけでは捉えにくい価値創造の絶対額を見る補助的な参考指標となり得る。

### Page 5

5/6 
資料 3 価値創造（EP：Economic Profit）の考え方

（出所）経済産業省「成長投資ガイダンス ―価値創造の拡大を通じた企業の持続的成長に向けて―」（2026 年）

EP において注目されるのは、ROIC を高めることにとどまらず、資本コストを超えて生み出され
る「価値の絶対額」に着目する点にある。ROIC が高い事業であっても、投下資本が小さければ、 当
該事業が生み出す EP の絶対額は小さくなり得る。また、成長余地のある事業において、追加投資
によってROIC がWACC を上回る状態を維持・拡大できる見込みがある場合には、 投下資本の拡大に
伴って EP の絶対額を拡大できる可能性がある。 一方で、 足元でROIC が高い事業だけに資本を集中
させることが、 必ずしも企業価値の極大化につながるとは限らない。 将来の成長余地や追加投資に
よる限界リターンの動向などを総合的に見極めることも肝要であろう。加えて、現時点で ROIC が
WACC を下回る事業についても、 直ちに撤退を決めるのではなく、 構造改革によって収益性を改善で
きる可能性を検討する余地がある。その際には、追加投資の規模や改革に要する期間・費用、他の
事業に経営資源を振り向けた場合の機会費用も踏まえて判断する必要がある。財務的な視点や定量
的なデータも踏まえ、事業戦略の妥当性を多角的に検証する取締役会での議論に期待したい。

4．独立社外取締役と取締役会事務局――実効性を支える基盤 
コードは、取締役会は社外取締役による問題提起を含め自由闊達で建設的な議論・意見交換を尊
ぶ気風の醸成に努めるべき旨を指摘している。取締役会が実効的な議論を展開するためには、 それ
を支える体制と環境の整備が不可欠である。独立社外取締役は、経営陣との間で緊張感のある信頼
関係を構築した上で、 経営陣に執行を委ねつつ、 監督や助言を通して取締役会における大局的な議
論や意思決定を行い、経営陣の説明が是認できない場合には、 反対意見を述べることも役割上必要
となる。監督と執行の間の情報の非対称性、監督と執行の境目の曖昧さ、さらには経営スピードと
監督の深さとのトレードオフなど、構造的な課題もある中で、独立社外取締役は、執行側から独立
した立場を保ちつつ、 業務執行の過度な細部に介入することなく、 適切な距離感を保って監督を行
うことが期待される。 
こうした課題に対して、 コードが言及する 「取締役会事務局 （コーポレートセクレタリー等） 」 の

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
機能強化が果たし得る役割は大きい。 強化された取締役会事務局による年間アジェンダの設計、 議
題の重要性に応じた審議時間の配分、取締役への情報提供、事前説明における論点整理、取締役会
での議論結果のフォローアップまでを一連のプロセスとして捉えることで、 取締役会の時間を企業
価値に直結する議論へ振り向ける余地が生まれる。 取締役会が個別案件の単なる承認作業から解放
され、企業戦略の方向付け、適切なリスクテイクの支援、内部統制・リスク管理などの重要テーマ
を踏まえた経営陣への実効的な監督に時間 を集中させることが可能となっているかが重要な論点
となる。 
例えば、独立社外取締役は、 「投資判断の前提となる市場成長率や競争優位性、期待ROIC は妥当
か」 「現在保有する事業を持ち続ける合理性はあるか」 「投資判断の前提が崩れた場合、いつ経営資
源の配分を見直すか」 といった問いを投げかけることができる。 こうした議論が経営資源の配分や
事業ポートフォリオの判断にどう影響したかは、 取締役会の実効性を考える上で重要な視点となる。
投資家と経営陣や社外取締役との対話においても、 「取締役会で何が議論され、どのように経営判
断に影響したか」という観点からの対話が視野に入る。

5．小括 
2026 年改訂 CG コードは、成長投資や事業ポートフォリオの見直しを含む経営資源の配分につい
て、 取締役会が戦略との整合性を継続的に検証することを重視している。 こうした役割を実効的に
果たすには、 独立社外取締役を含む取締役会の構成員が、 経営陣とは異なる視点から問いを投げか
け、議論を深めることが重要である。その結果、取締役会の意思決定プロセスそのものが重要とな
り、経営資源の配賦の規律について説明を求められる場面も増えていくことが想定される。 コード
の改訂を受けたコーポレートガバナンス報告書においても、監督と執行それぞれが果たす役割を踏
まえながら、どのように実効性のある取締役会運営がなされ得るのかについて、 説明の質が問われ
ることも考えられる。 
本コードと「スチュワードシップ・コード」は、車の両輪であり、両者が適切に相まって実効的
なコーポレートガバナンスが実現されることが期待される。コーポレートガバナンスの改善を強く
意識している長期保有の投資家は、コードの改訂を受けた開示を切り口に、取締役会が企業固有の
経営課題について問いを設定し、 その問いが持続的な企業価値向上にどう結び付いているかを確認
する機会を得ることも想定される。 
コード改訂を受けたコーポレートガバナンス報告書では、 成長戦略に沿って経営資源をどのよう
に配分し、 その妥当性を取締役会がいかに検証しているかについて、 各社の状況に即した説明が期
待される。原則を実施する場合にも、実施しない場合にも、その判断と具体的な取組みを丁寧に示
すことが、投資家との建設的な対話につながる。取締役会による監督の実効性が高まり、企業の成
長の道筋と資源配分の合理性を巡る対話が深まることを期待したい。

以上

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ

và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, triển vọng 
dài hạn phụ thuộc khả năng tham

tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang svck). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 tỷ 
VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự suy 
giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng mùa cao điểm hàng hóa cuối năm và mạng bay Trung Đông 
phục hồi sẽ hỗ trợ sản lượng SCS trong những tháng cuối năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ 
cải thiện còn hạn chế do nhu cầu vận tải trên các tuyến Trung Đông – châu Á và 
châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn suy yếu. Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự 
kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy).

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sau khi các hạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành được hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay quốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân Sơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh khoảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân bổ hàng quốc tế tại TP. Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 10%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì cổ tức tiền mặt cao nhờ khả năng tạo dòng 
tiền ổn định và quy mô tiền gửi lớn. Cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ 
phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027 nhằm tích lũy 
nguồn lực cho các dự án mới.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ hàng hóa Sài Gòn được thành 
lập năm 2008, hiện là đơn vị khai thác nhà ga 
hàng hóa hàng không lớn thứ 2 tại cảng 
hàng không quốc tế (HKQT) Tân Sơn Nhất 
với thị phần khoảng 50%. Các cổ đông lớn 
của SCS bao gồm CTCP Gemadept (33.4%) 
và Tổng Công ty Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam 
(13.6%). 
Hoạt động khai thác nhà ga là mảng kinh 
doanh chính của SCS. Hiện SCS đang tiếp 
tục mua sắm trang thiết bị nâng công suất 
xử lý hàng lên 350,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy) dù đã được hỗ 
trợ bởi mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi và mùa cao điểm cuối năm 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Thông lượng hàng hóa quốc tế qua SCS bắt đầu giảm từ 2027 với 10%/35%/55%/70% hàng quốc tế 
Tân Sơn Nhất phân bổ sang cho L TA vào 2027/2028/2029/2030, trước khi bắt đầu tăng trưởng trở lại từ 
2031. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định nhờ nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh, cổ tức 2026 
dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu và duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang yoy). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 
tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự 
suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

### Page 4

Biểu đồ 4. Cơ cấu thông lượng hàng SCS (tấn)  Biểu đồ 5. Giá dịch vụ và biên LNG hoạt động khai thác nhà ga 
của SCS

Nguồn: SCS  Nguồn: SCS

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS trong 2H2026, 
song mức độ cải thiện còn hạn chế 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng hàng hóa của SCS sẽ cải thiện trong những tháng 
cuối năm 2026 nhờ mạng bay Trung Đông dần được khôi phục, đặc biệt từ 
Qatar Airways, cùng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa tăng trong mùa cao điểm cuối 
năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ phục hồi dự kiến còn hạn chế khi nhu cầu vận tải trên 
các tuyến Trung Đông – Châu Á và Châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn giảm lần lượt 
14.1% và 16.1% yoy trong tháng 7/2026 (tháng thứ 5 liên tiếp), trái ngược với 
mức tăng 3.9% của thị trường hàng hóa hàng không toàn cầu. Diễn biến này 
phần nào giải thích việc sản lượng hàng quốc tế của SCS tiếp tục giảm khoảng 
10% yoy trong tháng 7–8, dù tần suất bay của các hãng Trung Đông đã phục 
hồi đáng kể.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng hàng quốc tế và nội địa trong bốn tháng cuối 
năm lần lượt giảm 4% và 2% yoy, cả năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy) và 
52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy). Doanh thu khai thác nhà ga được kỳ vọng tiếp tục 
được hỗ trợ bởi việc điều chỉnh tăng giá dịch vụ từ đầu năm, bù đắp một phần 
tác động từ sản lượng suy giảm.

Biểu đồ 6. Mức độ phục hồi chuyến bay của các hãng hàng không vùng Vịnh 
Đến ngày 21/09/2026, tần suất bay của các 
hãng vùng Vịnh đã phục hồi đáng kể, phần 
lớn đạt khoảng 85–100% mức trước xung đột, 
riêng Qatar Airways đạt 97%, tăng từ mức 
87% ghi nhận giữa tháng 6.
